+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+

+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+

     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),

+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }

+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;

+    ~CudaSimulation() {

+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);

+        cudaFree(material_indices_);

+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+

     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+
 // Validate simulation results
 bool validateResults(const World& world) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

+

+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+

 bool validateResults(const World& world) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

-                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
-            }

+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+

     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

+    CudaSimulation simulation(world);

+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace

+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));

+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \

+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};

+namespace {
+
+[[noreturn]] void failCuda(const cudaError_t error, const char* expression,
+                           const char* file, const int line) {
+    std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error at %s:%d (%s): %s\n", file, line,
+                 expression, cudaGetErrorString(error));
+    std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+}
+
+#define CUDA_CHECK(expression) \
+    do { \
+        const cudaError_t cuda_status = (expression); \
+        if (cuda_status != cudaSuccess) { \
+            failCuda(cuda_status, #expression, __FILE__, __LINE__); \
+        } \
+    } while (false)
+
+// Materials are read by every thread but never modified during a run.  Keeping
+// them in constant memory makes the material lookup a broadcast for a warp.
+__constant__ Material device_materials[3];
+
+// One thread computes one element.  The connectivity is stored in a
+// structure-of-arrays layout on the device, so neighboring threads issue
+// coalesced loads for each connection slot while retaining the original
+// connection order and accumulation order.
+__global__ void updateElements(
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ material_indices,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ num_connections,
+    const idx_t* __restrict__ connected_indices,
+    const val_t* __restrict__ connected_flux,
+    const ElementDynamic* __restrict__ current,
+    ElementDynamic* __restrict__ next,
+    const std::size_t n_elems) {
+    const std::size_t i = static_cast<std::size_t>(blockIdx.x) * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;
+    if (i >= n_elems) {
+        return;
+    }
+
+    const ElementDynamic this_element = current[i];
+    const Material material = device_materials[material_indices[i]];
+    val_t total_flux = material.external_flow;
+    const std::size_t connection_base = i * MAX_CONNECTIONS;
+
+    for (idx_t j = 0; j < num_connections[i]; ++j) {
+        const idx_t connection_offset = connection_base + j;
+        const idx_t neighbor_idx = connected_indices[connection_offset];
+        const val_t neighbor_energy = current[neighbor_idx].current_energy;
+        total_flux += (neighbor_energy - this_element.current_energy) *
+                      material.transfer_coeff * connected_flux[connection_offset] * 0.25;
     }
+
+    next[i].current_energy = this_element.current_energy + total_flux;
+    next[i].total_flux = this_element.total_flux + fabs(total_flux);
 }
 
+class CudaSimulation {
+  public:
+    explicit CudaSimulation(const World& world)
+        : n_elems_(world.elements_static.size()) {
+        int device_count = 0;
+        const cudaError_t device_count_status = cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count);
+        if (device_count_status != cudaSuccess) {
+            failCuda(device_count_status, "cudaGetDeviceCount(&device_count)", __FILE__, __LINE__);
+        }
+        if (device_count == 0) {
+            std::fprintf(stderr, "CUDA error: no CUDA device is available\n");
+            std::exit(EXIT_FAILURE);
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpyToSymbol(device_materials, world.materials.data(),
+                                      world.materials.size() * sizeof(Material)));
+
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&material_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&num_connections_),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_indices_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&connected_flux_),
+                              n_elems_ * MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[0]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&dynamic_[1]),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic)));
+
+        const char* static_data = reinterpret_cast<const char*>(world.elements_static.data());
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            material_indices_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, material_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            num_connections_, sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
+            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
+            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
+            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
+    }
+
+    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaSimulation() {
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
+        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
+        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
+        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
+        cudaFree(num_connections_);
+        cudaFree(material_indices_);
+    }
+
+    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
+    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
+    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
+    void run(const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
+        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
+            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
+        int current_buffer = 0;
+
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
+            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
+                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
+                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
+            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
+            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
+        }
+    }
+
+    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
+        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
+            return;
+        }
+
+        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
+        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
+                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
+    }
+
+  private:
+    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
+    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
+    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
+    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
+    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
+};
+
+}  // namespace
+

 bool validateResults(const World& world) {
     val_t energy_sum = 0.0;

   215	            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, num_connections), sizeof(ElementStatic),
   216	            sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   217	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
   218	            connected_indices_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t),
   219	            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_idx), sizeof(ElementStatic),
   220	            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(idx_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   221	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy2D(
   222	            connected_flux_, MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t),
   223	            static_data + offsetof(ElementStatic, connected_flux), sizeof(ElementStatic),
   224	            MAX_CONNECTIONS * sizeof(val_t), n_elems_, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   225	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(dynamic_[0], world.elements_dynamic.data(),
   226	                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));
   227	    }
   228	
   229	    CudaSimulation(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   230	    CudaSimulation& operator=(const CudaSimulation&) = delete;
   231	
   232	    ~CudaSimulation() {
   233	        cudaFree(dynamic_[0]);
   234	        cudaFree(dynamic_[1]);
   235	        cudaFree(connected_flux_);
   236	        cudaFree(connected_indices_);
   237	        cudaFree(num_connections_);
   238	        cudaFree(material_indices_);
   239	    }
   240	
   241	    // Run all iterations in the default stream.  Kernel launches in one
   242	    // stream are ordered, providing the global buffer-swap barrier required
   243	    // by the original Jacobi update without a per-element synchronization.
   244	    void run(const int n_iters) const {
   245	        if (n_elems_ == 0 || n_iters <= 0) {
   246	            return;
   247	        }
   248	
   249	        constexpr unsigned int threads_per_block = 256;
   250	        const unsigned int blocks = static_cast<unsigned int>(
   251	            (n_elems_ + threads_per_block - 1) / threads_per_block);
   252	        int current_buffer = 0;
   253	
   254	        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
   255	            updateElements<<<blocks, threads_per_block>>>(
   256	                material_indices_, num_connections_, connected_indices_, connected_flux_,
   257	                dynamic_[current_buffer], dynamic_[1 - current_buffer], n_elems_);
   258	            CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());
   259	            current_buffer = 1 - current_buffer;
   260	        }
   261	    }
   262	
   263	    void download(World& world, const int n_iters) const {
   264	        if (n_elems_ == 0) {
   265	            return;
   266	        }
   267	
   268	        const int current_buffer = (n_iters > 0) ? (n_iters & 1) : 0;
   269	        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(world.elements_dynamic.data(), dynamic_[current_buffer],
   270	                              n_elems_ * sizeof(ElementDynamic), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));
   271	    }
   272	
   273	  private:
   274	    std::size_t n_elems_ = 0;
   275	    idx_t* material_indices_ = nullptr;
   276	    idx_t* num_connections_ = nullptr;
   277	    idx_t* connected_indices_ = nullptr;
   278	    val_t* connected_flux_ = nullptr;
   279	    ElementDynamic* dynamic_[2] = {nullptr, nullptr};
   280	};
   281	
   282	}  // namespace
   283	
   284	// Validate simulation results
   285	bool validateResults(const World& world) {
   390	    World world;
   391	    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);
   392	    
   393	    // Calculate memory usage
   394	    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);
   395	    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;
   396	    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;
   397	    printf("Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n",
   398	           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   399	           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   400	           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   401	    printf("\n");
   402	    
   403	    // Allocate and populate the GPU state before timing the iteration loop.
   404	    // The benchmark's computation time therefore measures the CUDA workload,
   405	    // rather than one-time mesh upload and allocation costs.
   406	    CudaSimulation simulation(world);
   407	
   408	    // Run simulation
   409	    printf("Running simulation...\n");
   410	    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   411	    
   412	    simulation.run(n_iters);
   413	    simulation.download(world, n_iters);
   414	    
   415	    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
   416	    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();
   417	    
   418	    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration_ms);
   419	    
   420	    // Calculate performance metrics
   421	    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);
   422	    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;
   423	    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;
   424	    
   425	    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)
